# Target: fyers.in
# Current Phase: RECON (new surface mapped; advancing to SURFACE next)

## Key Findings
- **New in-scope host `mcp.fyers.in` — FYERS MCP (Model Context Protocol) SSE service.** `GET /mcp` → `text/event-stream` (200, holds open). An **unauthenticated JSON-RPC `initialize` POST returns server identity and capability schema** with no token: `Fyers Trading MCP Server v1.0.0`, `protocolVersion 2025-03-26`, capabilities `resources` + `tools(listChanged)`. `tools/list` without an SSE session → `Invalid session ID`; sessions require `Authorization: Bearer ${FIA_TOKEN}` (per `myapi.fyers.in/static/media/fia.*.yaml`). Root, `/health`, `/mcp.json`, `/.well-known/mcp` → 404 (19 B text). This is a **seventh auth domain** (FIA_TOKEN bearer) vs OAuth `access_token` — a fresh token-scoping/cross-service candidate.
- **New in-scope host `automate-chn.fyers.in`** (Cloudflare 104.18.4.135): `/automate/backtest/status` → `-16` trade-core; `/automate/backtest/` and root → 404. Second hostname serving the same automate backend as api-t2 — cross-host token-scoping candidate.
- **New auth fingerprint #7 confirmed:** `api-t2.fyers.in/quantainer/v1/charts/*` (`futures`, `atm-iv`, `multi-strike-iv`, `strategy`, …) → `{"code":-401,"message":"Please provide valid token"}` — message distinct from api-g1 `-401 Unauthorized`, i.e. another independent microservice/authz domain.
- **Full `api-t2/automate/*` + `osb/*` inventory recovered from public config** (correct base, not `/v1/`): `backtest/{history,preflight,start}`, `copy|details|draft?strategy_id=`, `explore`, `export`, `get-configuration?task_variant=MultilegOptions&task_id=PlaceOrder`, `get-nodes`, `my/*`, `schedule/*`, `strategy/pnl?strategy_id=`, `workflow/{stop,multi-stop}`, `workflows`, `tagpositionbook`, `osb/draft`. Sampled endpoints all `-16` auth-gated — object-ID (`strategy_id`) surfaces behind auth.
- **api-a1 auth split finalized:** `funds/v1/api/{send_otp,verify_otp,add_funds_status?order_id=,transaction_download_key}` → `-21`; `manage/account/{send_otp,verify_otp}` and `marina/v1/mtf/{send,verify}_otp` → `-16`. **No public OTP oracle exists on api-a1.**
- **api-i1 `fd/{plans,filters,overview}` uniformly `40101`; screeners.fyers.in/koshi/v2/* now `-16`** (an earlier run observed `/config` 200 — that surface has since been locked).
- **Auth-model intel (live docs, `myapi.fyers.in`):** v3.1 SEBI spec confirms refresh-token discontinuation, mandatory daily 2FA, static-IP whitelisting for order placement, single transactional App ID per user, MPP-forced market orders, no AMO; official API contact **`api-support@fyers.in`** (usable as disclosure contact). `fyers.in/{security,privacy,responsible-disclosure}` all 404.
- **Config drift:** `indus/v1/config` grew 113,497 → 114,456 B; current dump enumerates **870 `*.fyers.in` URLs**.

## API Endpoints
- `mcp.fyers.in/mcp` (POST/GET, unauthenticated `initialize` leaks serverInfo+capabilities; tools/session require Bearer FIA_TOKEN)
- `api-t2.fyers.in/quantainer/v1/charts/{futures,atm-iv,auto_atm_straddle,multi-strike-iv,multi_straddle_strangle,strategy,strategy/candles}` (auth `-401 valid token`)
- `api-t2.fyers.in/automate/*` (full path set from config; auth `-16`)
- `api-t2.fyers.in/osb/draft?strategy_id=` (404 at probe; route may live elsewhere)
- `api-a1.fyers.in/funds/v1/api/*` (auth `-21`), `manage/account/{send_otp,verify_otp}`, `marina/v1/mtf/*` (auth `-16`)
- `data.fyers.in/dev-fyers/savechart/1.2/charts` (500 invalid-method pre-auth; session-gated)
- `automate-chn.fyers.in/automate/backtest/status` (auth `-16`)

## Hypotheses
### H8: MCP (`mcp.fyers.in`) cross-domain authz + unauthenticated schema disclosure (NEW, MEDIUM-HIGH)
**Reasoning:** `initialize` is served unauthenticated and returns full server identity + capability flags; the real session/tools gate uses a separate `FIA_TOKEN` domain. If FIA_TOKEN scoping is implemented client-side or shared with OAuth `access_token` (the MCP package embeds a FYERS OAuth login), cross-domain privilege mixing is plausible. `tools/list` returns a hard error without session — a clean auth-boundary oracle.
**Impact:** info disclosure (5.3); tool-level ATO if FIA_TOKEN == access_token scoping overlaps (6.5–8.0 conditional).
**Read-only PoC design:** enumerate all JSON-RPC methods (`initialize`, `notifications/initialized`, `tools/list`, `resources/list`, `ping`) observing auth-gate vs protocol-gate error ordering; document error-shape table. No data-bearing calls.

### H9: `quantainer` + `automate-chn` token-scoping boundary (NEW, MEDIUM)
**Reasoning:** Distinct `-401 "valid token"` message and a second hostname serving `-16` trade-core both guard strategy/chart objects. A token issued by one domain's login may be honored by the other → cross-service authz.
**Read-only PoC design:** document-only; requires an authorized token. Record request template for a hypothetical api-t1 token against quantainer and automate-chn.

### H5 update: `strategy_id` / `order_id` / savechart object shapes (MEDIUM-HIGH)
Full config path set now known; `strategy_id=` and `order_id=` query objects map to `automate/*`, `osb/*`, `add_funds_status?order_id=` — all auth-gated; IDOR testing deferred until an authorized token path exists (H7 app-registration or own account).

## CVSS Candidates
- [H8 MCP unauthenticated identity/capability disclosure] — CVSS 5.3
- [H8 MCP FIA_TOKEN cross-domain scoping / tool ATO] — CVSS 6.5–8.0 (conditional)
- [H9 quantainer/automate-chn cross-host token scoping] — CVSS 6.5 (conditional)
- [H5 object-ID IDOR on automate/savechart/funds order_id] — CVSS 6.5–8.0 (behind auth)
- Carried forward: H1 OAuth code interception 8.1–9.0 (open redirect 6.1 CONFIRMED), H3 refresh-token/pin walk 7.5–9.0, H4 config disclosure 5.3, H2/H6/H7 6.1–8.0.

## CVSS Candidates
- [H1 OAuth code interception] — CVSS 8.1–9.0 (full ATO if code delivery confirmed)
- [H1 open-redirect / non-binding redirect_uri on generate-authcode] — CVSS 6.1 (confirmed step-1)

# 6 items on 2026-08-05 07:28:04 UTC
- **H1 step-A oracle matrix confirmed across 6 `redirect_uri` variants (read-only, live client `SOFG221ZX4-101`).** Registered (`pledge.fyers.in/index.html`), foreign-subdomain (`direct.fyers.in/auth/redirect`), external host (`evil.example.com/cb`), host-dot open-redirect (`pledge.fyers.inherit.example.com/cb`), and `?query#fragment` forms **all** render the identical **60,818-byte** login page. Byte-level and structural diffing of two fetches (same vs different URI) showed the only variance is per-request nonce/turnstile noise (~4 diff lines). **Conclusion: `generate-authcode` does NOT enforce an allowlist against the client's registered redirect_uri at step-1.** Only *presence* is validated.
- **Empty `redirect_uri` reveals the backend implementation detail.** Returns `302 → trade.fyers.in/api-login/error/index.html?error_msg=Key: 'AuthCodeRequest.RedirectURI' Error:Field validation for 'RedirectURI' failed on the 'required' tag`. This discloses: (a) the Go handler struct **`AuthCodeRequest.RedirectURI`**, (b) validation uses Go validator `required` tag (presence-only, not per-app registration). Strong PoC evidence for H1.
- **H3 field-walk oracle on `validate-refresh-token` proven (pre-auth, live).** Endpoint reachable without credentials and does progressive plaintext field validation: any `grant_type` other than `refresh_token` → `-442 "Please provide valid grant_type"`; `grant_type=refresh_token` advances to the next gate → `-501 "Please provide valid refresh token"`. Confirms a live refresh-token exchange surface with orderable validation branches.
- **SSO string-table cross-check** confirms backend token-family endpoints: `validate_refresh_token`, `validate_access_token`, `refresh_token_v2`, `REFRESH_TOKEN_VALIDITY`, `Bearer` header. `api/v3/validate-access-token` → 404 at that path (different route). `vagator/v2/validate_*` → 404.
- **Correction to H4:** the IDs leaked from `indus/v1/config` (`data_socket.cl_cug`, `analytics_exceptions.exceptional_clients`, `hsm_config.cug_mob`) are **7-char internal identifiers** (e.g. `XS53027`), NOT OAuth `client_id`s (real ones carry a `-101` appType suffix). Consequently they cannot be used to initiate an OAuth flow — H4 is downgraded to operational/internal identifier + feature-limit disclosure (still a publically reachable info-leak; reproducible 113,497-byte response, 73 endpoint blocks, 35 unique `*.fyers.in` hosts).
- **Auth-domain partitioning reconfirmed** (distinct, non-interoperable no-token fingerprints): `-16`/`-15` (api-t1/t2 trade+login core), `-401` (api-g1 settings), `40101` (api-i1 fixed-income), DRF `{"detail":...}` (verify_token_v2, guest_user/register). No token exists to bridge them → token-scoping remains design-only (a real api-t1 token is required to test api-g1 acceptance).

**Impact:** If refresh tokens remain honored post-deprecation or PIN/TOTP precedence is weak, token-rotation gaps could enable session/account takeover (CVSS 7.5–9.0).
## CVSS Candidates

# 5 items on 2026-08-05 10:14:51 UTC
- **H1 static deliverable complete: post-auth redirect destination is driven by client-side URL parameters.** Full deobfuscation of the SSO bundle (`login.min.js`, 310 KB, string-array obfuscation, 2 rotated arrays) shows the authenticated redirect handler reads `redirect_uri` **and a second alias `cb`** straight from the current page's query string and sets `location.href` to them (`if(_0x1b1ef9['redirect_uri']){location.href=…} else if(_0x1b1ef9['cb']){location.href=…}`), with only a 5-value reserved-`cb` denylist (`includes` check). The QR/WebSocket login success path pushes `_FYERS|<cookie>|_deviceId` (pipe-delimited) and then navigates to the URL-param destination. No server-side registry lookup on the destination is present anywhere in the flow. Combined with the confirmed step-1 oracle (Go `required`-tag, presence-only validation of `AuthCodeRequest.RedirectURI`), this closes the H1 PoC evidence chain for an **open-redirect-on-login primitive**; whether the code/cookie artifact is appended to the attacker URI can only be confirmed with authenticated testing (out of scope).
- **H3 complete field-walk on `validate-refresh-token` (pre-auth, live).** Progressive, orderable validation errors establish the exact request schema: `grant_type`≠`refresh_token`→`-442`; empty `refresh_token`→`-501`; missing `appIdHash`→`-371 "Please provide sha256 hash of appId and app secret"` (discloses the hashing scheme, matching the public `SHA256(appId:appSecret)`); missing `pin`→`-502 "Please provide your valid pin"`; all-present dummy values→`-501` (token validity gate last). Order: `grant_type → refresh_token → appIdHash → pin → token validity`. This is a clean pre-auth enumeration oracle for the refresh-token exchange and confirms v3 requires `pin` at the *validate* layer.
- **`/api/v3/token` refresh grant is fully opaque (`-16` uniform).** Unlike `validate-refresh-token`, the real token endpoint returns `-16` for every refresh grant permutation (missing/format-fine fields alike) — no field oracle. The two endpoints are separate services with divergent error behavior; any pin-requirement divergence between *validate* and *token* must be tested with a valid token (documented, not executed).
- **api-a1 splits into two independent auth domains.** `funds/v1/api/*` (40+ bank/mandate/withdrawal endpoints) → `-21 "Authorization token required"`; `manage/account/*` (90+ endpoints) → `-16`. Distinct from api-g1 (`-401`), api-i1 (`40101`), api-t2/baskets (`401 "missing authorization header"` plain text), api-t2/indri (`-16`), and DRF (`{"detail":"Not authenticated"}`). This yields ≥6 auth fingerprints across the estate — the core input to the H5 cross-service authz hypothesis.
- **Public config re-confirmed live** (113,497 B, 73 endpoint blocks): `api-a1.fyers.in` alone is referenced by **212 URLs** in the endpoint map, including sensitive objects (esign/initiate, selfie-validation, freeze/account, close-account/transfer-details, add_bank/penny_drop) — a stable recon artifact for further object-ID analysis.

Requires authenticated confirmation of the delivered artifact.

openapi: 3.0.0

info:
  description: |

    # Introduction

    FYERS MCP (Model Context Protocol) is the easiest way to sync your FYERS account with your AI platform of choice like Claude, Cursor, Windsurf and more.
    With one-tap installation.

    Your assistant instantly understands your trading context stocks, mutual funds, positions, orders, GTTs, option chains, alerts, live and historic data.

    No generic answers. No setup hassle. Just context-aware insights, built on your FYERS data. Tailor-made for expert traders and investors. 

    # What is FYERS MCP?

    Every AI tool is only as good as the context it has. FYERS MCP injects your portfolio, trades, and market data directly into your assistant, so it can respond with answers personalised to you.
    
    For traders and investors, this means natural-language queries like “What’s my P&L this week?” or “Show me my exposure in banking stocks.”

    For developers, this means direct access to a raw MCP server schema—so you can integrate, extend, and build custom workflows on top of FYERS.
    
    MCPs are your way to sync FYERS context into your AI, making every response sharper, faster, and more useful.

    # Why FYERS MCP is Better

    Most MCPs are clunky, limited, and built only for developers. **FYERS MCP** is built for *everyone*—giving you all the power others offer, plus more.

    | Feature / Experience | FYERS MCP 🚀 | Others ❌ |
    |-----------------------|--------------|-----------|
    | **Installation**      | One-tap (.exe / .pkg) | Multi-step, config heavy |
    | **Ease of Use**       | Simple, no coding needed | Geared towards devs only |
    | **Market Data**       | Live + Historic prices | Often live only |
    | **Portfolio Coverage** | Stocks, MFs, trades, orders, GTT, alerts | Limited holdings/trades |
    | **Options & F&O**     | Full option chain | Partial or missing |
    | **Developer Value**   | Raw MCP schema + server specs to build custom tools | MCP Server Schema and Specs |
    | **Audience**          | Investors + Developers | Mostly developers |
    | **Coverage**          | Equities, Derivatives, MFs | Equities, Derivatives, MFs |

    ---

    With **FYERS MCP**, you’re not choosing between ease and depth—you get both.

    # How to Install

    FYERS MCP is packaged for **Mac (.pkg)** and **Windows (.exe)**. Just download, run, log in, and authorise. Your AI platform instantly syncs with your FYERS context—no command line, no code edits.

    For everyday users, that means you’re ready in minutes.  
    For developers, it means the installation doubles up as a foundation: the raw server spec is there if you want to extend MCP into your own custom setup.

    Getting started with FYERS MCP is effortless


    # For Mac & Windows

    1. Download the installer for **Mac (.pkg)** or **Windows (.exe)**.  
    2. Run the installer, it will install Node.js and Claude Desktop.  
    3. Restart Claude Desktop and log in to your FYERS account.  
    4. Authorise once, and your AI platform is synced with your FYERS context.  

    👉 No coding. No command line. Just click, connect, and go.  

    MacOS installer <a href="https://assets.fyers.in/mcp/macos/1.0.0/FYERS-Claude-MCP-Installer-v1.0.0.pkg">Click here</a>
    
    Windows installer AMD <a href="https://assets.fyers.in/mcp/windows/1.0.0/FYERS-Claude-MCP-Installer-v1.0.0.exe">Click here</a>
    
    _(Note:Supported versions: Windows 10, Windows 11 & above and MacOS Big Sur & above)_

    ## For Cursor

    1. Open **Cursor**.  
    2. Go to **Settings → MCP & Integrations**.  
    3. Click **Add New MCP Server**.  
    4. Paste the following JSON and save
    
    ```json
    {
        "mcpServers": {
            "FIA-MCP": {
                "url": "https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp",
                "type": "sse",
                "headers": {
                    "Authorization": "${FIA_TOKEN}"
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ```

    5. Config done! Cursor is now connected to FYERS MCP, just restart cursor. 
    6. Installer is supported only in macOS Version 14 & above and Windows 11 & above.

    # Use Cases That Add Value

    FYERS MCP turns your AI into a true **market co-pilot**:

    - **Portfolio Review** – Ask for today’s P&L, your best performers, or a sector-wise breakdown.  
    - **Market Monitoring** – Summarise index moves, track sector rotation, or flag unusual volume.  
    - **Options & Derivatives** – Get the full option chain, analyse strategies, or calculate payoffs.  
    - **Orders & GTT** – Check all your orders and trades.  
    - **Historic Insights** – Pull price history, compare performance to benchmarks, or backtest strategies.  
    - **Alerts** – Create alerts that notify you when markets move.  
    - **Developer Edge** – Plug MCP into your own tools, automate reports, or build strategy testers on top of the raw server schema.

    Every use case translates into added value: less friction, more clarity, and better decisions in less time.

    # Security & Privacy

    Your account data is secure, always.

    - **On Your Terms** – MCP only works with permissions you allow.  
    - **No Shared Credentials** – Authentication stays with FYERS' secure login flow, never through the AI.  
    - **Encrypted by Default** – Every request is end-to-end encrypted; keys and tokens stay on your device.  
    - **Transparent History** – All MCP actions—data pulls, alerts, trades—are logged, so you’re always in control.  

    Security isn’t an add-on. It’s part of the design.

    # The FYERS Advantage

    Other MCPs unlock access. **FYERS MCP unlocks context.**  
    It brings your portfolio and market context into your AI, so insights aren’t just accurate they’re actionable.  

    And whether you’re a retail investor who wants simplicity or a developer who wants raw control, FYERS MCP is built for you.  

    👉 **Download FYERS MCP today and make your AI truly market-smart.**

import zlib
data=open('mcp.pkg','rb').read()
# xar: header 'xar!' then 28-byte header; find compressed TOC (usually deflate)
print("magic:", data[:4])
# TOC offset/size in header
import struct
if data[:4]==b'xar!':
    header_size=struct.unpack('>H',data[4:6])[0]
    toc_len=struct.unpack('>Q',data[8:16])[0]
    toc_c_len=struct.unpack('>Q',data[16:24])[0]
    print("header_size",header_size,"toc_len",toc_len,"toc_comp_len",toc_c_len)
    toc_comp=data[header_size:header_size+toc_c_len]
    try:
        toc=zlib.decompress(toc_comp)
        print("TOC decompressed",len(toc))
        open('toc.xml','wb').write(toc)
    except Exception as e:
        print("decomp err",e)
EOF

import zlib, re
def dec(b):
    # xar gzip-style: 4-byte BE length prefix + zlib stream (or raw deflate)
    tries=[("zlib full",lambda: zlib.decompress(b)),
           ("zlib[4:]",lambda: zlib.decompress(b[4:])),
           ("raw[4:]",lambda: zlib.decompress(b[4:], -15)),
           ("raw full",lambda: zlib.decompress(b, -15)),
           ("gzip full",lambda: zlib.decompress(b, 16+zlib.MAX_WBITS))]
    for name,fn in tries:
        try:
            d=fn(); return name,d
        except Exception: pass
    return "none", None
for name in ["xar_Distribution","xar_PackageInfo","xar_welcome_html","xar_license_html","xar_Bom"]:
    b=open(name,'rb').read()
    m,d=dec(b)
    print("====",name,"method",m)
    if d:
        print(d[:600].decode(errors='replace'))
EOF

0707070000000000000407550246311013350000050000001506642567300000200000000000. 0707070000000000011007550246311013350000010000001506642567300001600000010157./postinstall #!/bin/bash

# Get the actual user who invoked the installer (not root)
ACTUAL_USER=$(stat -f "%Su" /dev/console)
ACTUAL_USER_HOME=$(eval echo "~$ACTUAL_USER")
LOGFILE="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/fyers_claude_installer.log"

echo "Starting Claude Desktop installation process for user: $ACTUAL_USER" > "$LOGFILE"
echo "User home directory: $ACTUAL_USER_HOME" >> "$LOGFILE"

# Install Node.js if not already installed
echo "Checking Node.js installation..." >> "$LOGFILE"
SCRIPT_DIR="$(dirname "$0")"
/bin/bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/install_nodejs.sh"

# Path to check if Claude Desktop is installed
APP_PATH="/Applications/Claude.app"

if [ -d "$APP_PATH" ]; then
    echo "Claude is already installed." >> "$LOGFILE"
else
    echo "Claude not found. Downloading..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    
    # URL to the Claude DMG
    DMG_URL="https://storage.googleapis.com/osprey-downloads-c02f6a0d-347c-492b-a752-3e0651722e97/nest/Claude.dmg"
    
    # Path to save the downloaded DMG
    DMG_PATH="/tmp/Claude.dmg"
    
    # Download the DMG
    curl -o "$DMG_PATH" "$DMG_URL" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    # Mount the DMG
    MOUNT_POINT="/Volumes/ClaudeInstaller"
    echo "Mounting DMG..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    hdiutil attach "$DMG_PATH" -mountpoint "$MOUNT_POINT" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    # Copy the app to /Applications
    echo "Copying to Applications folder..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    cp -R "$MOUNT_POINT/Claude.app" /Applications/ >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    # Set proper ownership and permissions for Claude.app
    echo "Setting proper ownership for Claude.app..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    chown -R root:admin /Applications/Claude.app >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    chmod -R 755 /Applications/Claude.app >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    # Unmount the DMG
    echo "Unmounting DMG..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    hdiutil detach "$MOUNT_POINT" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    # Cleanup
    echo "Cleaning up..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    rm "$DMG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    echo "Installation complete: Claude Desktop." >> "$LOGFILE"
fi

# Pre-create Claude directories with proper ownership and permissions
echo "Setting up Claude Desktop directories and permissions..." >> "$LOGFILE"
CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/Library/Application Support/Claude"
CLAUDE_LOGS_DIR="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/Library/Logs/Claude"
CLAUDE_CACHES_DIR="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/Library/Caches/Claude"

# Create all necessary directories
mkdir -p "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
mkdir -p "$CLAUDE_LOGS_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1  
mkdir -p "$CLAUDE_CACHES_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

# Set proper ownership (user:staff is standard on macOS)
chown -R "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chown -R "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_LOGS_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chown -R "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_CACHES_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

# Set proper permissions (755 for directories, 644 for files)
chmod -R 755 "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chmod -R 755 "$CLAUDE_LOGS_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chmod -R 755 "$CLAUDE_CACHES_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

echo "✅ Claude Desktop directories created with proper permissions" >> "$LOGFILE"

# Configure MCP (run as the actual user, not root)
echo "Configuring MCP..." >> "$LOGFILE"
SCRIPT_DIR="$(dirname "$0")"

# Try to run as user first, fallback to direct execution
echo "Attempting to run MCP configuration as user $ACTUAL_USER..." >> "$LOGFILE"
if sudo -u "$ACTUAL_USER" /bin/bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/configure_mcp.sh" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1; then
    echo "✅ MCP configuration completed via sudo -u" >> "$LOGFILE"
else
    echo "⚠️  sudo -u failed, trying direct execution..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    # Set environment variables for the script
    export SUDO_USER="$ACTUAL_USER"
    if /bin/bash "$SCRIPT_DIR/configure_mcp.sh" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1; then
        echo "✅ MCP configuration completed via direct execution" >> "$LOGFILE"
    else
        echo "❌ MCP configuration failed" >> "$LOGFILE"
        exit 1
    fi
fi

# Final permission check and fix
echo "Final permission verification..." >> "$LOGFILE"
chown -R "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chown "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$LOGFILE" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

echo "✅ Installation completed successfully for user: $ACTUAL_USER" >> "$LOGFILE"

exit 0
0707070000000000021007550246311013350000010000001506642567300002400000006331./install_nodejs.sh #!/bin/bash

# Install Node.js LTS if not already installed

# Determine the actual user and home directory
if [ -n "$SUDO_USER" ]; then
    # Running via sudo, use the original user
    ACTUAL_USER="$SUDO_USER"
    ACTUAL_USER_HOME=$(eval echo "~$SUDO_USER")
elif [ "$USER" != "root" ]; then
    # Running as regular user
    ACTUAL_USER="$USER"
    ACTUAL_USER_HOME="$HOME"
else
    # Fallback: get the console user
    ACTUAL_USER=$(stat -f "%Su" /dev/console)
    ACTUAL_USER_HOME=$(eval echo "~$ACTUAL_USER")
fi

LOGFILE="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/fyers_claude_installer.log"

echo "Checking Node.js installation for user: $ACTUAL_USER..." >> "$LOGFILE"

# Check if node is installed in various locations
check_node_installation() {
    # Check using command -v (checks PATH)
    if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        NODE_PATH=$(command -v node)
        NODE_VERSION=$(node --version)
        echo "Node.js found via PATH: $NODE_PATH ($NODE_VERSION)" >> "$LOGFILE"
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check using which
    if which node >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        NODE_PATH=$(which node)
        NODE_VERSION=$($NODE_PATH --version)
        echo "Node.js found via which: $NODE_PATH ($NODE_VERSION)" >> "$LOGFILE"
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check common installation locations
    COMMON_PATHS=(
        "/usr/local/bin/node"
        "/opt/homebrew/bin/node"
        "/usr/bin/node"
    )
    
    for NODE_PATH in "${COMMON_PATHS[@]}"; do
        if [ -x "$NODE_PATH" ]; then
            NODE_VERSION=$($NODE_PATH --version 2>/dev/null)
            if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
                echo "Node.js found at: $NODE_PATH ($NODE_VERSION)" >> "$LOGFILE"
                return 0
            fi
        fi
    done
    
    # Check NVM installations
    if [ -d "$HOME/.nvm/versions/node" ]; then
        for NVM_NODE_PATH in "$HOME/.nvm/versions/node"/*/bin/node; do
            if [ -x "$NVM_NODE_PATH" ]; then
                NODE_VERSION=$($NVM_NODE_PATH --version 2>/dev/null)
                if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
                    echo "Node.js found via NVM: $NVM_NODE_PATH ($NODE_VERSION)" >> "$LOGFILE"
                    return 0
                fi
            fi
        done
    fi
    
    return 1
}

if check_node_installation; then
    echo "Node.js is already installed, skipping installation." >> "$LOGFILE"
    exit 0
fi

echo "Node.js not found. Installing LTS version..." >> "$LOGFILE"

# Download and install Node.js LTS
NODE_PKG_URL="https://nodejs.org/dist/v20.18.0/node-v20.18.0.pkg"
NODE_PKG_PATH="/tmp/node-installer.pkg"

echo "Downloading Node.js LTS..." >> "$LOGFILE"
curl -o "$NODE_PKG_PATH" "$NODE_PKG_URL" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "❌ Failed to download Node.js" >> "$LOGFILE"
    exit 1
fi

echo "Installing Node.js..." >> "$LOGFILE"
installer -pkg "$NODE_PKG_PATH" -target / >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "✅ Node.js installation completed" >> "$LOGFILE"
    # Cleanup
    rm "$NODE_PKG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
    
    # Verify installation
    if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        NODE_VERSION=$(node --version)
        echo "✅ Node.js verified: $NODE_VERSION" >> "$LOGFILE"
    fi
else
    echo "❌ Node.js installation failed" >> "$LOGFILE"
    exit 1
fi

exit 0
0707070000000000031007550246311013350000010000001506642567300002300000016742./configure_mcp.sh #!/bin/bash

# Configure Claude Desktop MCP Server for FYERS

# Determine the actual user and home directory
if [ -n "$SUDO_USER" ]; then
    # Running via sudo, use the original user
    ACTUAL_USER="$SUDO_USER"
    ACTUAL_USER_HOME=$(eval echo "~$SUDO_USER")
elif [ "$USER" != "root" ]; then
    # Running as regular user
    ACTUAL_USER="$USER"
    ACTUAL_USER_HOME="$HOME"
else
    # Fallback: get the console user
    ACTUAL_USER=$(stat -f "%Su" /dev/console)
    ACTUAL_USER_HOME=$(eval echo "~$ACTUAL_USER")
fi

LOGFILE="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/fyers_claude_installer.log"

echo "Configuring Claude Desktop MCP server for user: $ACTUAL_USER..." >> "$LOGFILE"
echo "Using home directory: $ACTUAL_USER_HOME" >> "$LOGFILE"

# Find Node.js installation
find_node_path() {
    # Check using command -v (checks PATH)
    if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        command -v node
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check using which
    if which node >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        which node
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check common installation locations
    COMMON_PATHS=(
        "/usr/local/bin/node"
        "/opt/homebrew/bin/node"
        "/usr/bin/node"
    )
    
    for NODE_PATH in "${COMMON_PATHS[@]}"; do
        if [ -x "$NODE_PATH" ]; then
            echo "$NODE_PATH"
            return 0
        fi
    done
    
    # Check NVM installations
    if [ -d "$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/.nvm/versions/node" ]; then
        for NVM_NODE_PATH in "$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/.nvm/versions/node"/*/bin/node; do
            if [ -x "$NVM_NODE_PATH" ]; then
                echo "$NVM_NODE_PATH"
                return 0
            fi
        done
    fi
    
    return 1
}

NODE_CMD=$(find_node_path)
if [ -z "$NODE_CMD" ]; then
    echo "❌ Node.js not found, cannot configure MCP" >> "$LOGFILE"
    exit 1
fi

echo "Using Node.js at: $NODE_CMD" >> "$LOGFILE"

# Claude config directory and file paths
CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR="$ACTUAL_USER_HOME/Library/Application Support/Claude"
CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH="$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/claude_desktop_config.json"

echo "Target config directory: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE"
echo "Target config file: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE"

# Create Claude config directory with proper ownership and permissions
echo "Creating Claude config directory..." >> "$LOGFILE"
sudo -u "$ACTUAL_USER" mkdir -p "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

# Verify directory was created
if [ ! -d "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" ]; then
    echo "❌ Failed to create Claude config directory" >> "$LOGFILE"
    exit 1
fi

# Set proper ownership and permissions
chown -R "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chmod 755 "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

echo "✅ Claude config directory created and configured" >> "$LOGFILE"

# MCP configuration to add
MCP_CONFIG='{
  "mcpServers": {
    "FYERS-MCP": {
      "command": "npx",
      "args": [
        "mcp-remote",
        "https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp"
      ]
    }
  }
}'

# Check if Claude config already exists
if [ -f "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" ]; then
    echo "-> Found existing Claude Desktop config, merging..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    
    # Read existing config and merge with MCP config using Node.js
    if sudo -u "$ACTUAL_USER" "$NODE_CMD" -e "
const fs = require('fs');

try {
    // Read existing config
    let claudeConfig = {};
    try {
        const data = fs.readFileSync('$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH', 'utf8');
        claudeConfig = JSON.parse(data);
        console.error('Existing config loaded successfully');
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Existing config corrupted or not found, creating new one...');
        claudeConfig = {};
    }

    // Add or update MCP servers
    if (!claudeConfig.mcpServers) {
        claudeConfig.mcpServers = {};
    }

    // Add FYERS-MCP server
    claudeConfig.mcpServers['FYERS-MCP'] = {
        command: 'npx',
        args: ['mcp-remote', 'https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp']
    };

    // Write updated config
    fs.writeFileSync('$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH', JSON.stringify(claudeConfig, null, 2));
    console.error('MCP configuration merged successfully');
} catch (error) {
    console.error('Error:', error.message);
    process.exit(1);
}
" 2>> "$LOGFILE"; then
        echo "✅ MCP configuration merged with existing Claude Desktop config" >> "$LOGFILE"
    else
        echo "❌ Failed to merge MCP configuration" >> "$LOGFILE"
        exit 1
    fi
else
    echo "-> Creating new Claude Desktop config..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    echo "   Directory: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE"
    echo "   Config file: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE"
    
    # Create new config with MCP server using sudo -u to run as the actual user
    echo "   Writing MCP configuration as user $ACTUAL_USER..." >> "$LOGFILE"
    if sudo -u "$ACTUAL_USER" bash -c "cat > '$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH' << 'EOFCONFIG'
{
  \"mcpServers\": {
    \"FYERS-MCP\": {
      \"command\": \"npx\",
      \"args\": [
        \"mcp-remote\",
        \"https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp\"
      ]
    }
  }
}
EOFCONFIG" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1; then
        echo "✅ New Claude Desktop config created with MCP server" >> "$LOGFILE"
    else
        echo "❌ Failed to create Claude Desktop config file" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   Attempting alternative method..." >> "$LOGFILE"
        
        # Alternative method: create as root then change ownership
        if cat > "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" << 'EOFCONFIG'
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "FYERS-MCP": {
      "command": "npx",
      "args": [
        "mcp-remote",
        "https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp"
      ]
    }
  }
}
EOFCONFIG
        then
            chown "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
            echo "✅ Claude Desktop config created using alternative method" >> "$LOGFILE"
        else
            echo "❌ Failed to create config file with alternative method" >> "$LOGFILE"
            exit 1
        fi
    fi
fi

# Final ownership and permissions check
echo "Setting final permissions on config file..." >> "$LOGFILE"
chown "$ACTUAL_USER:staff" "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1
chmod 644 "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1

# Verify the file was created and has content
if [ -f "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" ]; then
    FILE_SIZE=$(stat -f%z "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    if [ "$FILE_SIZE" -gt 0 ]; then
        echo "✅ MCP configuration successfully added to Claude Desktop" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   Config file: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   File size: $FILE_SIZE bytes" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   File owner: $(stat -f '%Su:%Sg' "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" 2>/dev/null || echo 'unknown')" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   File permissions: $(stat -f '%Mp%Lp' "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" 2>/dev/null || echo 'unknown')" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   File contents preview:" >> "$LOGFILE"
        head -10 "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1 || echo "   Could not read file contents" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "🎉 CONFIGURATION COMPLETE!" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   ✅ Claude Desktop will now connect to FYERS MCP server" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   ✅ Restart Claude Desktop to activate the FYERS integration" >> "$LOGFILE"
        echo "   📍 Config location: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE"
    else
        echo "❌ Config file created but is empty (size: $FILE_SIZE)" >> "$LOGFILE"
        exit 1
    fi
else
    echo "❌ Config file was not created at: $CLAUDE_CONFIG_PATH" >> "$LOGFILE"
    echo "   Directory contents:" >> "$LOGFILE"
    ls -la "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" >> "$LOGFILE" 2>&1 || echo "   Could not list directory" >> "$LOGFILE"
    exit 1
fi

exit 00707070000000000040000000000000000000000010000000000000000000001300000000000TRAILER!!!                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         

import json,re
d=json.load(open('cfg.json'))
cur=d['endpoints']['current']
hosts=set(); urls=set()
def walk(o):
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for v in o.values(): walk(v)
    elif isinstance(o,list):
        for i in o: walk(i)
    elif isinstance(o,str):
        for u in re.findall(r'https?://([a-z0-9.-]*fyers\.in)[a-zA-Z0-9/_.?=&%-]*', o):
            hosts.add(u)
        for u in re.findall(r'https?://[a-z0-9.-]*\.fyers\.in[a-zA-Z0-9/_.?=&%-]*', o):
            urls.add(u)
walk(cur)
print("hosts:", len(hosts))
for h in sorted(hosts): print("  ",h)
print("total urls:", len(urls))
# count urls per host
from collections import Counter
c=Counter()
for u in urls:
    m=re.match(r'https?://([a-z0-9.-]+fyers\.in)', u)
    if m: c[m.group(1)]+=1
print("\nurls per host:")
for h,n in c.most_common(): print(f"  {n:5d}  {h}")

+# SURFACE round 2026-08-05 20:xx UTC
+- **H8 boundary oracle finalized — `mcp.fyers.in` auth is enforced ONLY at POST dispatch, not on the SSE stream.** Full JSON-RPC walk (no token): `initialize` → 200 identity+capabilities (only unauth method); `notifications/initialized`, `tools/list`, `tools/call`, `resources/list`, `resources/templates/list`, `prompts/list`, `ping` → all `Invalid session ID`. `GET /mcp` returns 200 `text/event-stream` holding open with a bogus `Authorization: Bearer` (no sessionId event, no error, no auth check) — the stream open is unauthenticated, session gate lives at request dispatch. `.well-known/oauth-*`, `/oauth`, `/mcp/oauth` all 404 → OAuth metadata not exposed server-side (native flow via client).
+- **MCP macOS `.pkg` statically dissected (assets.fyers.in/mcp/macos/1.0.0/FYERS-Claude-MCP-Installer-v1.0.0.pkg, 22,175 B xar).** Contains: `Distribution`/`PackageInfo` (`com.fyers.claudeinstaller`, postinstall), `welcome/license.html`, and a 15,872 B gzip `postinstall` bundle with 3 shell scripts. Behaviour: downloads Node.js v20.18.0 and Claude.dmg, then writes `~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json` with `{"mcpServers":{"FYERS-MCP":{"command":"npx","args":["mcp-remote","https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp"]}}}`. **No token/scopes embedded** — auth is native MCP OAuth (`mcp-remote`), confirming H8 relies purely on the FIA_TOKEN bearer at the SSE endpoint. Windows .exe is 77,235,112 B (not pulled; noted for later static analysis if needed).
+- **api-i1 split into four independent auth domains (fingerprints #9–#11).** `investment/*/fd/*` → `40101 "Authorization token is required"`; `mf/data/v2/*` gated set → `-101 "Authorization token is required"`; `mf/txn/v1/*` → `-101 "Authorisation token is missing."` (message variant); `investment/tapi/v1/ipo/v2/*` → `-100 "Authorisation token required."`. Four distinct error schemes under one hostname ⇒ per-service authz domains.
+- **NEW: raw Microsoft SQL Server error leak (unauthenticated) on `api-i1.fyers.in/mf/data/v2/scheme/details/chart`.** With valid `period` (1M/1Y/3M/5Y; `1D` is invalid) returns `{"Number":1103,"SQLState":[52,50,48,48,48],"Message":"Incorrect table name ''"}` — SQLState decodes to "42000". Backend is SQL Server; the chart table name is interpolated server-side (empty for unknown params), and the raw DB error is reflected with no wrapper and **no auth**. isin/plan_id/symbol params accepted but do not set the table name → table name derived server-side. Error-based oracle + stack disclosure on a public endpoint (H10, CVSS 4.3 baseline; SQLi conditional on table-name provenance).
+- **Public `mf/data/v2` data tier enumerated (api-i1).** No-auth 200: `home` (plan_id/ISIN/NAV/AUM/expense_ratio/ratings of top funds), `assortments` (category-wise index values), `collateral` (funds eligible as margin incl. haircut), `explore/filters` (rating/risk/AMC enum), `scheme/details/about?isin=` (fund objective), `scheme/details/holding-allocation?isin=` (equity/debt split, sector allocation), `scheme/details/chart` (SQL leak). Gated `-101`: `scheme/details/overview`, `get_order_window`, `redeem-ow`, `sip_schedule_dates`. Auth-gate ordering is inconsistent per-endpoint (validation-before-auth on `about`; auth-before-params on `overview`).
+- **NEW: `api.fyers.in/fy/cdsl/dev/*` pre-auth input-validation surface.** `{cdslEdis,edis,pledge}/{index,details,tpin,userdata,setup,status,reqdetails}` respond with input errors ("Invalid request method" on GET, "Invalid Input Format" on POST `{}`) — **no auth fingerprint at all** (no `-16`/`-401`/`-21`). The non-`dev` twins (`/fy/cdsl/cdslEdis/index` etc.) return the standard session-gate `500 "Invalid Request, please provide valid method"`. ⇒ a dev-stage EDIS/TPIN (electronic-delivery-instruction / pledge) service on the prod `api.fyers.in` host validates input before any observable auth; EDIS + TPIN = securities-transfer primitive (H11, behind input schema unknown; PoC = request-schema field-walk read-only).
+- **`vagator/v2/get_tradecast_token` is a pre-auth GET oracle** (`{"s":"error","code":-1044,"message":"invalid input"}` for any/no param; auth header ignored) — tradecast websocket-token endpoint, param schema not yet recovered from any JS bundle (absent from trade.js/fyers_common_modules.js).
+- **`watson-obs/v1/telemetry`** (api-t2) POST → `-99 "unauthorised access token"` — auth fingerprint #8; GET → 404.
+- **Config-drift deltas vs prior dump:** new quantainer surface `quantainer/v1/oi_stats/{change,drops,historical_pcr_max_pain,interpretation,max_pain,multi_strike,spurts,time_vs_oi,trending}` all `-401 "Please provide valid token"`; new `alpha/v1/client` → 404; `osb/my` and `indus/history/greeks/real-time` → `-16`; `automate-chn.fyers.in/automate/backtest/{cancel,result,status,trade-book-export,trade-book}` (all `-16`). ## Key Findings
- **Raw SQL Server error leak (unauthenticated)** on `api-i1.fyers.in/mf/data/v2/scheme/details/chart`: valid `period` values (1M/1Y/3M/5Y) return `{"Number":1103,"SQLState":[52,50,48,48,48],"Message":"Incorrect table name ''"}` (SQLState = "42000"). Backend = MS SQL Server; chart table name interpolated server-side, raw DB error reflected with no wrapper and no auth. New H10.
- **api.fyers.in `fy/cdsl/dev/*` pre-auth input-validation surface** (H11): `{cdslEdis,edis,pledge}/{index,details,tpin,userdata,setup,status}` return input errors ("Invalid request method"/"Invalid Input Format") with **no auth fingerprint**, while non-dev twins return the session-gate `500`. Dev-stage EDIS/TPIN (securities-transfer) service validates before auth on prod.
- **MCP .pkg dissected**: 22 KB xar → postinstall writes Claude config `npx mcp-remote https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp`; no embedded token/scopes. H8 relies purely on FIA_TOKEN at the SSE endpoint; auth enforced only at POST dispatch (`initialize` is the sole unauth method).
- **api-i1 = 4 auth domains**: `40101` (fd), `-101 "Authorization token is required"` (mf/data gated), `-101 "Authorisation token is missing."` (mf/txn), `-100 "Authorisation token required."` (tapi IPO).
- **Public mf/data tier**: home/assortments/collateral/explore-filters/about/holding-allocation public; overview/get_order_window/redeem-ow/sip_schedule_dates gated.
- **Auth fingerprint #8**: `watson-obs/v1/telemetry` POST → `-99 "unauthorised access token"`.
- Config drift: +`quantainer/v1/oi_stats/*` (-401), `osb/my`/`indus/history/greeks/real-time` (-16), automate-chn backtest set (-16); `api-t1-cdn screeners` now Cloudflare-503.

## API Endpoints
- `api-i1.fyers.in/mf/data/v2/scheme/details/chart?period=1Y` (GET, no-auth) — SQL error oracle
- `api.fyers.in/fy/cdsl/dev/{cdslEdis,edis,pledge}/*` (POST, no auth-fingerprint) — input-validation oracle
- `api-i1.fyers.in/mf/data/v2/scheme/details/{about,holding-allocation}?isin=` (GET, public)
- `api-t2.fyers.in/vagator/v2/get_tradecast_token` (GET, `-1044`)
- `api-t2.fyers.in/watson-obs/v1/telemetry` (POST, `-99`)

## Hypotheses
### H10: SQL error-based oracle on public MF chart endpoint
**Reasoning:** valid period reaches SQL with empty interpolated table name; raw Number/SQLState/Message reflected unauthenticated. **Impact:** stack/DB-engine disclosure + error oracle (4.3); SQLi conditional on whether any param (e.g. plan_id/isin/period) feeds the table-name/query (not yet demonstrated). **Test (read-only):** benign value classes → record error-shape table; no injection payloads.
### H11: cdsl/dev EDIS/TPIN validation-before-auth
**Reasoning:** dev-stage service on prod host validates input with zero auth fingerprint, non-dev twin session-gated. **Impact:** if any `dev` path reaches pledge/EDIS data without auth → 6.5–7.5; else 5.3 (schema disclosure). **Test (read-only):** field-walk POST schema via error-shape diffing; no real client/PAN/TPIN.
### H9 (carried): token-scoping table now complete (document-only; needs authorized token).

- [H11 cdsl/dev pre-auth input-validation surface] — CVSS 5.3–7.5 (conditional)

# Target: fyers.in
# Current Phase: SURFACE (IN_PROGRESS)

## Key Findings
- **H10 closed (downgraded).** `scheme_code` is the chart table selector — `scheme_code=47941&period=1Y` returns real public NAV history; strings/nonexistent codes → empty `nav_history[]` (parameterized, **no injection**). The raw SQL-1103 error only fires on the plan_id-only/`scheme_code`-absent fallback path with an empty interpolated table name. ⇒ engine disclosure + benign error oracle, no SQLi. CVSS 3.9–4.1.
- **H11 cdsl/dev route-validity oracle mapped.** Three-way discriminator: valid path+method → `"Invalid Input Format"`; wrong method → `"Invalid request method"`; unknown resource → `"Invalid resource"`. Valid routes: `cdslEdis/{index,details,tpin}`, `edis/{index,details,tpin}` (POST JSON); `pledge/{userdata,status,reqdetails}`, `unpledge/status` (GET); `pledge/setup`/`unpledge/setup` (POST). **No progressive field oracle** — every body shape returns uniform error, no auth fingerprint. Schema disclosure only. CVSS 5.5 (downgraded).
- **`api/beta/*` dead backend** — uniform 502 across all 8 routes (GET/POST). `api-y1/trade/v3/*` → 404 (Apache Test Page host, config entries don't resolve).
- **New auth fingerprint #12:** `api-i1/fy/mf/v1/holdings` → `-15 "Could not authenticate the user"` (trade-core domain on api-i1, distinct from its 40101/-101/-100).
- **`vagator/v2/guest_user/login_v2`** → `-1024 "please provide a valid mobile number"` (validation-before-auth phone oracle, pre-auth).
- Confirmed stable: api-a1 send_otp 404, api-g1 `-401`, quantainer `-401`, `indus/v1/config` live (now with `ab_feature_set` product matrix).

## API Endpoints
- `/mf/data/v2/scheme/details/chart?scheme_code={int}&period={1M,3M,6M,1Y,3Y,5Y}` (GET, no-auth) — public NAV history; SQL-1103 error on plan_id-only path
- `/fy/cdsl/dev/{cdslEdis,edis}/{index,details,tpin}` (POST), `/fy/cdsl/dev/{pledge}/{userdata,status,reqdetails}` (GET), `unpledge/status` (GET) — route/method oracle, no auth
- `/api/beta/*` (any method) — 502 dead
- `/vagator/v2/guest_user/login_v2` (POST) — `-1024` phone-validation oracle
- `/fy/mf/v1/holdings` (api-i1) — `-15` trade-core auth gate

## Hypotheses
### H10: chart SQL error-shape oracle (resolved — downgraded)
**Reasoning:** table name is selected by `scheme_code` only; param is parameterized. **Impact:** SQL Server engine/error-shape disclosure on public endpoint (3.9–4.1); no query-control demonstrated.
### H11: cdsl/dev EDIS/TPIN validation-before-auth (resolved — downgraded)
**Reasoning:** dev-stage routes validate input with no auth fingerprint, but no key-branching exists to walk the schema and no data path is reachable pre-auth. **Impact:** route/method schema disclosure (5.5). Any real impact needs a valid client/TPIN → behind auth.
### H1 (carried): open redirect / OAuth code interception on login host — remains the top candidate.

## CVSS Candidates
- [H1 OAuth code interception] — CVSS 8.1–9.0 (conditional on code-delivery to attacker URI)
- [H1 open redirect on login host] — CVSS 6.1 (confirmed step-1)
- [H8 MCP FIA_TOKEN scoping / tool ATO] — CVSS 6.5–8.0 (conditional)
- [H11 cdsl/dev schema disclosure] — CVSS 5.5 (downgraded)
- [H8 MCP unauth identity/capability disclosure] — CVSS 5.3
- [H10 chart SQL error oracle] — CVSS 3.9–4.1 (downgraded)

import json,re
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
t={int(k):v for k,v in json.load(open('string_table.json')).items()}
# replace ANY _0xNAME(0xHEX) where 0xHEX in table
def repl(m):
    idx=int(m.group(1),16)
    if idx in t: return json.dumps(t[idx])
    return m.group(0)
code=re.sub(r"_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}\((0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)\)", repl, s)
open('sso.deob2.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(code)
print('remaining decoder calls:', len(re.findall(r"_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}\(0x", code)))
# find the redirect logic block: if(...['redirect_uri']) location.href=...
i=code.find("'redirect_uri'")
# search for the 'redirect_uri' + 'cb' combined navigation logic
import re
for m in re.finditer(r"'redirect_uri'", code):
    seg=code[max(0,m.start()-600):m.start()+600]
    if "'cb'" in seg and 'href' in seg:
        print("=== NAV LOGIC ===")
        print(seg)
        print("==========")

[0m$ [0mcd /tmp/opencode && python3 << 'EOF'
import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()

# 1. find array producer literals: function _0xARR(){var _0xY=[...];...;return _0xY;}
arrays={}
pat_arr = re.compile(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(\[[^;]{0,20000}?\]);")
for m in pat_arr.finditer(s):
    name=m.group(1); lit=m.group(3)
    try:
        arr=json.loads(lit)
        arrays[name]=arr
    except Exception as e:
        # maybe contains decoder calls -> raw
        arrays[name]=lit
print("array producers found:", len(arrays))
print("names:", sorted(arrays.keys()))

# 2. decoders: _0xDEC ... offset ... uses array producer
# from earlier: map decoder->(array,offset)
decmap={}
pat_dec = re.compile(r"var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\);return \1=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{[^=]{0,40}?=([^;]{1,20})-0x([0-9a-f]+);")
for m in pat_dec.finditer(s):
    decm map=None
# looser scan
for m in re.finditer(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=function\([^)]*\)\{var [^=]+=([^;]+?)\(\);return \1=function\([^)]*\)\{[^=]{0,40}?=([^;]{1,25})-0x([0-9a-f]+);", s):
    dec=m.group(1); arrname=m.group(2); off=int(m.group(4),16)
    if arrname not in arrays: continue
    decmap[dec]=(arrname,off)
print("decoders mapped:", len(decmap))

json.dump({"arrays":{k:v for k,v in arrays.items() if isinstance(v,list)}, "decmap":{k:[v[0],v[1]] for k,v in decmap.items()}}, open('tables2.json','w'))
for d,(a,o) in list(decmap.items())[:20]:
    print(d,a,hex(o))

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()

arrays={}
pat_arr = re.compile(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(\[[^;]{0,20000}?\]);")
for m in pat_arr.finditer(s):
    name=m.group(1); lit=m.group(3)
    try:
        arr=json.loads(lit)
        arrays[name]=arr
    except Exception:
        arrays[name]=lit
print("array producers found:", len(arrays))
print("names:", sorted(arrays.keys()))

decmap={}
for m in re.finditer(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=function\([^)]*\)\{var [^=]+=([^;]+?)\(\);return \1=function\([^)]*\)\{[^=]{0,40}?=([^;]{1,25})-0x([0-9a-f]+);", s):
    dec=m.group(1); arrname=m.group(2); off=int(m.group(4),16)
    if arrname in arrays:
        decmap[dec]=(arrname,off)
print("decoders mapped:", len(decmap))

json.dump({"arrays":{k:v for k,v in arrays.items() if isinstance(v,list)}, "decmap":{k:[v[0],v[1]] for k,v in decmap.items()}}, open('tables2.json','w'))

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()

# array producers: function _0xPROD(){var _0xY=[...];
prods={}
for m in re.finditer(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(\[[^;]{0,20000}?\]);", s):
    name=m.group(1); lit=m.group(3)
    try: prods[name]=json.loads(lit)
    except Exception: prods[name]=None
print("array producers:", len(prods))

# decoders: function _0xDEC(a,b){var c=_0xPROD();return _0xDEC=function(a2,b2){a2=a2-0xOFF;var d=c[a2];return d;},_0xDEC(a,b);}
decmap={}
for m in re.finditer(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\);return \1=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})-0x([0-9a-f]+);var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=\4\[\6\];return \10;\},\1\(\2,\3\);}", s):
    dec=m.group(1); prod=m.group(5); off=int(m.group(9),16)
    if prod in prods and prods[prod] is not None:
        decmap[dec]=(prod,off)
    else:
        decmap[dec]=(prod,off)

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()

# load decoded master table (index->string) for array _0xbcc9 (offset 0x1b7)
master=json.load(open('string_table.json'))
master_by_idx={int(k):v for k,v in master.items()}

def extract_array(src):
    m=re.search(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=\[", src)
    if not m: return None,None
    prod=m.group(1); start=m.end()
    depth=1; i=start; instr=False; esc=False
    while i<len(src) and depth>0:
        c=src[i]
        if instr:
            if esc: esc=False
            elif c=='\\': esc=True
            elif c=='"': instr=False
        else:
            if c=='"': instr=True
            elif c=='[': depth+=1
            elif c==']': depth-=1
        i+=1
    arr_txt=src[start:i-1]
    # placeholder decoder calls
    place={}
    def ph(m):
        name=m.group(1); idx=m.group(2)
        tok=f"__DEC_{len(place)}__"
        place[tok]=(name,int(idx,16))
        return '"'+tok+'"'
    arr_txt2=re.sub(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((0x[0-9a-f]+)\)", ph, arr_txt)
    try:
        arr=json.loads('['+arr_txt2+']')
    except Exception as e:
        print("json err for",prod,e)
        return prod,None
    return prod,(arr,place)

prods={}
# iterate: strip previously found producer, find next
tmp=s
while True:
    prod,res=extract_array(tmp)
    if not prod: break
    prods[prod]=res
    # remove this producer's function body region
    idx=tmp.find("function "+prod+"()")
    # skip to end of array to avoid re-matching same
    tmp=tmp[:idx]+tmp[idx+300000:]
    if len(prods)>25: break
print("producers:",len(prods))
for k,v in prods.items():
    print(k,"entries:", len(v[0]) if v else None)

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
master=json.load(open('string_table.json'))
master_by_idx={int(k):v for k,v in master.items()}

def parse_js_array(arr_txt):
    # replace single-quoted strings with double-quoted (properly escaped)
    out=[]; i=0; n=len(arr_txt)
    def read_str(quote):
        j=i; s=[]; 
        while j<n:
            c=arr_txt[j]
            if c=='\\':
                s.append(c); s.append(arr_txt[j+1] if j+1<n else ''); j+=2; continue
            if c==quote:
                s.append(c); return ''.join(s), j+1
            s.append(c); j+=1
        return ''.join(s), n
    while i<n:
        c=arr_txt[i]
        if c in "'\"":
            raw,j=read_str(c)
            out.append(raw); i=j
        elif c==',' or c=='[' or c==']' or c==' ':
            # decoder call token or spacing
            out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c.isdigit() or c=='-':
            # number token (rare)
            j=i
            while j<n and (arr_txt[j].isalnum() or arr_txt[j] in '.-'):
                j+=1
            out.append(arr_txt[i:j]); i=j
        else:
            # decoder call like _0x..(0x..)
            j=i
            while j<n and arr_txt[j] not in ",[] \t\n":
                j+=1
            out.append(arr_txt[i:j]); i=j
    return ''.join(out)

def extract_all(src):
    prods={}
    tmp=src
    while True:
        m=re.search(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=\[", tmp)
        if not m: break
        prod=m.group(1); start=m.end()
        depth=1; i=start; instr=False; esc=False; q=None
        while i<len(tmp) and depth>0:
            c=tmp[i]
            if instr:
                if esc: esc=False
                elif c=='\\': esc=True
                elif c==q: instr=False
            else:
                if c in "'\"": instr=True; q=c
                elif c=='[': depth+=1
                elif c==']': depth-=1
            i+=1
        arr_txt=tmp[start:i-1]
        arr_txt2=parse_js_array(arr_txt)
        place={}
        def ph(m):
            name=m.group(1); idx=m.group(2)
            tok=f"__DEC_{len(place)}__"
            place[tok]=(name,int(idx,16))
            return '"'+tok+'"'
        arr_txt3=re.sub(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((0x[0-9a-f]+)\)", ph, arr_txt2)
        try:
            arr=json.loads('['+arr_txt3+']')
        except Exception as e:
            print("json err for",prod,e)
            arr=None
        prods[prod]=(arr,place)
        idx=tmp.find("function "+prod+"()")
        tmp=tmp[:idx]+tmp[idx+len(prod)+len("function ()"):]  # won't fully remove; use char skip
        # robust: cut the matched region
        tmp=tmp[:m.start()]+tmp[i:]
    return prods

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
arrays=json.load(open('arrays_captured.json'))
master=json.load(open('string_table.json'))
master_by_idx={int(k):v for k,v in master.items()}
# master producer _0xbcc9 offset 0x1b7
# build decmap
pat=re.compile(r"function (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\);return \1=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})-0x([0-9a-f]+);var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=\4\[\6\];return \10;\},\1\(\2,\3\);}")
decmap={}
for m in pat.finditer(s):
    dec=m.group(1); prod=m.group(5); off=int(m.group(9),16)
    decmap[dec]=(prod,off)
print("decoders:",len(decmap))
# map producer->array, master= _0xbcc9 -> use master table
arrays['_0xbcc9']=[master_by_idx.get(0x1b7+i) for i in range(len(master))]
# resolve
def resolve(dec, idx):
    prod,off=decmap.get(dec,('_0xbcc9',0x1b7))
    arr=arrays.get(prod)
    if arr is None:
        return f"??{dec}({hex(idx)})"
    p=idx-off
    if 0<=p<len(arr):
        v=arr[p]
        return v
    # try master fallback
    if idx in master_by_idx: return master_by_idx[idx]
    return f"??{dec}({hex(idx)})"
# deobfuscate
def repl(m):
    dec=m.group(1); idx=int(m.group(2),16)
    return json.dumps(resolve(dec,idx))
code=re.sub(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)\)", repl, s)
open('sso.full.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(code)
print("remaining unresolved calls:", len(re.findall(r"\?\?", code)))
print("len",len(code))
# Check cb/redirect navigation logic
import re
for m in re.finditer(r"'cb'", code):
    seg=code[max(0,m.start()-500):m.start()+400]
    if "'redirect_uri'" in seg or 'location' in seg or 'href' in seg:
        print("=== NAV SEGMENT ===")
        print(seg.replace(';',';\n')[:1400])
        print("========")
        break

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
arrays=json.load(open('arrays_captured.json'))
master=json.load(open('string_table.json'))
master_by_idx={int(k):v for k,v in master.items()}

# decmap via 'return _0xDEC=function(' marker
decmap={}
for m in re.finditer(r"return (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{", s):
    name=m.group(1); start=m.start()
    seg=s[start:start+320]
    offm=re.search(r"-0x([0-9a-f]+);", seg)
    arrm=re.search(r"var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)", s[max(0,start-400):start])
    off=int(offm.group(1),16) if offm else None
    prod=arrm.group(2) if arrm else None
    decmap[name]=(prod,off)
print("decoders:",len(decmap))
for d,(p,o) in sorted(decmap.items()): print(" ",d,p,hex(o) if o is not None else None)

arrays['_0xbcc9']=[master_by_idx.get(0x1b7+i,'') for i in range(len(master))]
def resolve(dec, idx):
    prod,off=decmap.get(dec,('_0xbcc9',0x1b7))
    if prod is None: return f"??{dec}"
    arr=arrays.get(prod)
    if arr is None:
        return f"??{dec}({hex(idx)})"
    p=idx-off
    if 0<=p<len(arr):
        v=arr[p]
        if isinstance(v,str): return v
        return str(v)
    if idx in master_by_idx: return master_by_idx[idx]
    return f"??{dec}({hex(idx)})"

def repl(m):
    dec=m.group(1); idx=int(m.group(2),16)
    return json.dumps(resolve(dec,idx))
code=re.sub(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)\)", repl, s)
open('sso.full.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(code)
print("unresolved markers:", len(re.findall(r"\?\?", code)))
print("len",len(code))

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
arrays=json.load(open('arrays_captured.json'))
master=json.load(open('string_table.json'))
master_by_idx={int(k):v for k,v in master.items()}

decmap={}
for m in re.finditer(r"return (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{", s):
    name=m.group(1); start=m.start()
    seg=s[start:start+320]
    offm=re.search(r"-0x([0-9a-f]+);", seg)
    arrm=re.search(r"var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)", s[max(0,start-400):start])
    off=int(offm.group(1),16) if offm else None
    prod=arrm.group(2) if arrm else None
    decmap[name]=(prod,off)

# for master, the array is arrays['_0xbcc9'] (captured) -> use captured, offset 0x1b7
def resolve(dec, idx):
    prod,off=decmap.get(dec,('_0xbcc9',0x1b7))
    if prod is None: return f"??{dec}"
    arr=arrays.get(prod)
    if arr is None: return f"??{dec}({hex(idx)})"
    p=idx-off
    if 0<=p<len(arr) and isinstance(arr[p],str): return arr[p]
    if idx in master_by_idx: return master_by_idx[idx]
    return f"??{dec}({hex(idx)})"

def repl(m):
    dec=m.group(1); idx=int(m.group(2),16)
    return json.dumps(resolve(dec,idx))
code=re.sub(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)\)", repl, s)
open('sso.full.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(code)
print("unresolved (alias/decoys):", len(re.findall(r"\?\?", code)))
print("len",len(code))

# extract full nav handler segment
m=re.search(r"var _0x53965f=_0x236e43", code)
if m:
    seg=code[m.start():m.start()+1400]
    print("=== NAV HANDLER ===")
    print(seg.replace(';',';\n')[:1800])

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
arrays=json.load(open('arrays_captured.json'))
master=json.load(open('string_table.json'))
master_by_idx={int(k):v for k,v in master.items()}
alias=json.load(open('alias.json'))

decmap={}
for m in re.finditer(r"return (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=function\((_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}),(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\)\{", s):
    name=m.group(1); start=m.start()
    seg=s[start:start+320]
    offm=re.search(r"-0x([0-9a-f]+);", seg)
    arrm=re.search(r"var (_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\(\)", s[max(0,start-400):start])
    off=int(offm.group(1),16) if offm else None
    prod=arrm.group(2) if arrm else None
    decmap[name]=(prod,off)

def canon(dec):
    seen=set()
    while dec in alias and dec not in seen:
        seen.add(dec); dec=alias[dec]
    return dec

def resolve(dec, idx):
    dec=canon(dec)
    prod,off=decmap.get(dec,('_0xbcc9',0x1b7))
    arr=arrays.get(prod)
    if arr is not None:
        p=idx-off
        if 0<=p<len(arr) and isinstance(arr[p],str): return arr[p]
    if idx in master_by_idx: return master_by_idx[idx]
    return None

def repl(m):
    dec=m.group(1); idx=int(m.group(2),16)
    v=resolve(dec,idx)
    if v is None: return m.group(0)
    return json.dumps(v)
code=re.sub(r"(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})\((0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)\)", repl, s)
open('sso.full.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(code)
print("unresolved decoder calls:", len(re.findall(r"_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8}\(0x", code)))
print("len",len(code))

# now extract nav handlers cleanly
import re
for m in re.finditer(r"location\[[\"']href[\"']\]=", code):
    seg=code[max(0,m.start()-600):m.start()+150]
    if "'cb'" in seg or "'redirect_uri'" in seg:
        print("=== location.href= seg ===")
        print(seg.replace(';',';\n'))
        print("======")
        break

import re, json
s=open('sso.js',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
known_decs=set(['_0x178fe0','_0x1afeb9','_0x1bbf4e','_0x287550','_0x2a51c6','_0x2deb1d','_0x382781','_0x3a87ca','_0x3b3815','_0x419818','_0x4fb0e9','_0x501658','_0x52c838','_0x539478','_0x55fcb0','_0x5d29','_0x5e058e','_0x9c72a'])
alias={}
for m in re.finditer(r"(?:var |,)(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})[,;]", s):
    a,b=m.group(1),m.group(2)
    if a not in known_decs and b!=a:
        alias[a]=b
# also _0xX=_0xY standalone after } 
for m in re.finditer(r"}(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8})=(_0x[0-9a-f]{4,8});", s):
    a,b=m.group(1),m.group(2)
    if a not in known_decs and b!=a:
        alias[a]=b
# transitive closure canonical
def canon(dec):
    seen=set()
    while dec in alias and dec not in seen:
        seen.add(dec); dec=alias[dec]
    return dec
# how many chains terminate at a known decoder?
n_ok=0; bad=set()
for a,b in list(alias.items()):
    c=canon(a)
    if c in known_decs: n_ok+=1
    else: bad.add(c)
print("aliases:",len(alias),"chains to known decoder:",n_ok)
print("bad terminals:", list(bad)[:10])
json.dump(alias, open('alias.json','w'))
# specifically check the nav aliases
print("_0x46e61a ->", canon('_0x46e61a'))
print("_0x145a83 ->", canon('_0x145a83'))
print("_0x28f892 ->", canon('_0x28f892'))
print("_0x1f610f ->", canon('_0x1f610f'))
print("_0x551fa4 ->", canon('_0x551fa4'))

import re
s=open('step1.html',encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').read()
print("len", len(s))
# scripts
for m in re.finditer(r'<script[^>]*src="([^"]+)"', s):
    u=m.group(1)
    if 'login' in u or 'sso' in u: print("SSO script:", u)
# hidden inputs (login form fields)
for m in re.finditer(r'<input[^>]*>', s):
    tag=m.group(0)
    if 'hidden' in tag or 'redirect_uri' in tag or 'client_id' in tag or 'state' in tag or 'code_challenge' in tag:
        print(tag)
# any reflection of redirect_uri / evil.example
print("evil.example reflected:", s.count('evil.example.com'))
print("redirect_uri present:", s.count('redirect_uri'))

# Target: fyers.in
# Current Phase: POC (advanced one phase from HYPOTHESIS)

## Key Findings
- **H1 POC evidence chain closed (this run).** Fully deobfuscated the *current* `login.min.js?v=1.3` (18 string arrays + 91-alias map resolved; 0 unresolved calls) and extracted the exact reserved-`cb` denylist, the post-auth navigation branch order, and the complete OAuth code-delivery path.
- **Reserved-`cb` denylist (5 values, exact-match):** `https://fyers.in/web`, `https://fyers.in/web/`, `https://fyers.in/web/markets/home`, `https://trade.fyers.in`, `https://trade.fyers.in/`. Handler: `if(cb && DENYLIST.includes(cb)) delete cb`. Any other `cb`/`redirect_uri` value is honored.
- **Post-auth branch order (static):** `redirect_uri` → `cb` → `(client_id&&response_type&&state)` OAuth → default page. `params()` reads `new URLSearchParams(location.search)` — all attacker-controlled in a phished URL.
- **Code-delivery path (static):** `LoginClient.redirection_url = params.redirect_uri` → POST `/api/v2/direct-login` (body `{app_id, redirect_uri, user_id, auth, state, scope, nonce, response_type}`) → `handleDirectLoginResponse`: `location.href = data.Url`. **Server `Url` is the code carrier; whether it embeds `auth_code` + registry-validates `redirect_uri` is the sole remaining unknown.**
- **PKCE ineffective:** attacker controls `code_challenge`/verifier in the phished URL (step-1 accepts it — live-confirmed with `code_challenge=abc123def456`).
- **Step-1 re-confirmed live on current build:** `generate-authcode?client_id=SOFG221ZX4-101&redirect_uri=https://evil.example.com/cb&...&code_challenge=...` → HTTP 200, 60,917 B login page, no allowlist rejection, no URI reflection.
- **Step-1 inline `cb` handler is UI-only** (`startsWith` radio pre-check), not a security gate — does not constrain the post-auth `location.href=cb`.

## API Endpoints
- `GET /api/v3/generate-authcode?client_id={id}&redirect_uri={hostile}&response_type=code&state={s}&code_challenge={attacker}` (api-t1, no auth) — step-1 oracle: 200 login page, URI accepted
- `POST https://api.fyers.in/api/v2/direct-login` / `POST https://api-t1.fyers.in/api/v3/direct-login` — direct-login exchange; response `data.Url` → `location.href` (code carrier, unverified)
- `POST /api/v3/validate-authcode` / `POST /api/v3/token` — v3 code/token redemption (documented shapes, prior runs)

## Hypotheses
### H1: OAuth `redirect_uri`/`cb` code interception + open redirect — POC DESIGN COMPLETE
**Reasoning (evidence chain A→D):** (A) step-1 accepts arbitrary URI live; (B) `getQueryStringValue()` feeds `redirect_uri`/`cb` from `location.search`; (C) `LoginClient` forwards `params.redirect_uri` into the direct-login POST; (D) server `Url` → `location.href`. **Impact:** open redirect from trusted login host (6.1); if `data.Url` embeds the code, attacker-in-loop code redemption → full trading-account ATO (8.1–9.0). **PoC design (documented, read-only):** step-1 curl above → victim logs in on the authentic page → client-side JS navigates to attacker URI; success indicators: (1) post-auth navigation/302 to attacker URI (open redirect), (2) `auth_code` present in `data.Url` of direct-login response (code interception). **Proof needed:** authenticated confirmation of (2) by the program.

## CVSS Candidates
- [H1 open redirect on login host] — CVSS 6.1 (CONFIRMED step-1 + static navigation primitive)
- [H1 OAuth code interception → ATO] — CVSS 8.1–9.0 (conditional: auth_code in `data.Url`; needs authenticated confirmation)
- [H8 MCP FIA_TOKEN scoping / tool ATO] — CVSS 6.5–8.0 (conditional; next to firm up)
- [H11 cdsl/dev schema disclosure] — CVSS 5.5 (downgraded) · [H8 MCP identity disclosure] — 5.3 · [H10 chart SQL error oracle] — 3.9–4.1

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');

// Get the proper npx path for Windows
function getNpxCommand() {
  if (process.platform === 'win32') {
    const nodejsPath = process.env.ProgramFiles ? path.join(process.env.ProgramFiles, 'nodejs') : 'C:\\Program Files\\nodejs';
    return path.join(nodejsPath, 'npx.cmd');
  }
  return 'npx';
}

const MCP_CONFIGURATION = {
  mcpServers: {
    'FYERS-MCP': {
      command: getNpxCommand(),
      args: ['mcp-remote', 'https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp']
    }
  }
};

class MCPConfigurator {
  constructor(logger) {
    this.logger = logger;
    const appData = process.env.APPDATA || path.join(os.homedir(), 'AppData', 'Roaming');
    this.configDirectory = path.join(appData, 'Claude');
    this.configPath = path.join(this.configDirectory, 'claude_desktop_config.json');
  }

  async ensureConfiguration() {
    // Fix npm directory issue on Windows first
    if (process.platform === 'win32') {
      await this.fixNpmDirectories();
    }

    await fs.promises.mkdir(this.configDirectory, { recursive: true });

    let existingConfig;
    if (fs.existsSync(this.configPath)) {
      try {
        const raw = await fs.promises.readFile(this.configPath, 'utf8');
        existingConfig = JSON.parse(raw);
        await this.backupExistingConfig();
      } catch (error) {
        this.logger.warn('Failed to parse existing Claude config, creating backup', { error: error.message });
        await this.createInvalidBackup();
      }
    }

    const merged = this.mergeConfig(existingConfig || {});
    await fs.promises.writeFile(this.configPath, JSON.stringify(merged, null, 2), 'utf8');
    this.logger.info('Claude MCP configuration updated', { configPath: this.configPath });

    return {
      status: 'updated',
      configPath: this.configPath
    };
  }

  mergeConfig(existing) {
    const merged = { ...existing };
    merged.mcpServers = {
      ...(existing.mcpServers || {}),
      ...MCP_CONFIGURATION.mcpServers
    };
    return merged;
  }

  async backupExistingConfig() {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    const backupPath = `${this.configPath}.${timestamp}.bak`;
    await fs.promises.copyFile(this.configPath, backupPath);
    this.logger.info('Created Claude config backup', { backupPath });
  }

  async createInvalidBackup() {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    const backupPath = `${this.configPath}.${timestamp}.invalid`;
    try {

[0m$ [0mcd /home/runner/work/fyers-recon/fyers-recon && cat >> findings_deepseek.md << 'EOF'

# POC round 2026-08-06 07:xx UTC — H1 direct-login pre-auth field-walk (v2 vs v3) + api-i1 invest/admin schema oracle + indus/user domain + Windows MCP installer pass
- **H1 extension: pre-auth field-walk on BOTH direct-login variants (read-only, spaced).** `POST api.fyers.in/api/v2/direct-login` (AWS ALB, no WAF): validates `scope` before token. Any non-empty scope string → `{"s":"error","code":-443,"message":"Please provide a valid scope"}` (401) for both real (`SOFG221ZX4-100`) and synthetic app_ids ⇒ registry-derived allowlist. Empty/absent `scope` → advances past the scope gate → `-17 "Could not authenticate the user"` (401). Body `redirect_uri: https://evil.example.com/cb` is **not rejected as malformed** at any gate — it is accepted into the exchange request; only the scope/token gates stop the walk (registry binding of redirect_uri still not observable pre-auth; requires authenticated confirmation). `POST api-t1.fyers.in/api/v3/direct-login` (Cloudflare): **auth-first**, `-441 "invalid auth token"` (400) regardless of scope — no `-443` scope gate, divergent validation order vs v2. Note: `-441` is the same numeric code previously seen as "auth code required" on validate-authcode — the numeric code space is shared across services with different semantics. New codes added to estate map: `-17` (v2 direct-login auth), `-441` (v3 direct-login invalid auth token).
- **H12 (new): live `/invest/admin/` surface on api-i1 with pre-auth required-parameter schema oracle.** `GET api-i1.fyers.in/invest/admin/v1/sgb/issue-list` — admin-prefixed path on prod. Three-gate presence walk before auth: missing `issue_id` → `-19 "issue_id is missing in request"` (422); +`is_active` → `-19 "is_active is missing in request"`; +`source` → **`-19 "souce is missing in request"`** (internal var name `souce` leaked via typo); all present → auth gate `-27 "Could not authenticate the user"` (401). `issue_id=abc` (non-numeric) passes presence gate → auth ⇒ presence-only validation, type-check deferred post-auth. Full admin-tree probe: only `sgb/issue-list` resolves (`-18` 404 on ncd/fds/etf/us-equity/reits/bond/schemes/root). **Auth fingerprint #13: `-27`** on `invest/admin/*` (distinct from api-i1 `-15`/`-100`/`-101`/`40101`). **False-positive triage:** SmartHunt's claimed SSTI on this endpoint is unsupported — `${7*7}` returned CF HTTP 429 (rate-limit block page), not a template-evaluated reflection; do NOT report SSTI. Real (low) issues: admin-path schema disclosure + presence oracle + auth-domain split.
- **H13 (new): `indus/user/*` per-user chart-settings/gallery/profile — auth domain #14 + IDOR candidate behind auth.** `api-t1.fyers.in/indus/user/v1/{gallery,chart-settings,user-profile}` → `-15 "Please provide valid token"` (401; message variant distinct from quantainer `-401 "Please provide valid token"`). `savechart`/`option-settings` → 404 at probed bases. `savedcharts.fyers.in` (React SPA) authenticates via `_FYERS` cookie → extracts `tokenHash` from JWT `at_hash` (atob'd) → GET/DELETE `indus/user/v1/gallery`. Per-user saved-chart gallery ⇒ IDOR candidate behind auth (chart id / user enumeration). Confirms `login.fyers.in/?cb=https://savedcharts.fyers.in` is a legitimate non-denylisted cb — FYERS-internal yet outside the 5-value exact-match denylist, consistent with the H1 primitive. `insights.fyers.in` → `/fundamentals/` widgets fetch only `public.fyers.in/sym_details/*.json` (public data); `tradingview.fyers.in` = no A record (dead).
- **H8 firmed up (static, Windows installer): no embedded credentials in either installer.** NSIS self-extracting exe (requireAdministrator) → `app-64.7z` (76 MB) → Electron Claude Desktop + installer source. `mcpConfig.js` writes `%APPDATA%\Claude\claude_desktop_config.json` merging `{"mcpServers":{"FYERS-MCP":{"command":"<PF>\\nodejs\\npx.cmd","args":["mcp-remote","https://mcp.fyers.in/mcp"]}}}`; existing config backed up (`.bak`/`.invalid`); uninstall deliberately preserves the config (Delete commented out → leftover-config residue). Downloads: Claude Desktop from official Anthropic `storage.googleapis.com/osprey-downloads-…`, Node.js from `nodejs.org`. Auto-update channel GitHub `fyers/claude-installer` (app-update.yml; noted as disclosure/recon artifact, not enumerated per scope). **No FIA_TOKEN / client_secret / OAuth scope anywhere** in Mac .pkg or Windows .exe ⇒ H8's only live surfaces remain: unauth `initialize` identity/capability disclosure (CVSS 5.3, confirmed) and FIA_TOKEN cross-tool scoping (conditional — requires an authenticated FIA_TOKEN; documented, not executed).
EOF
